# Metroidvanias: the complete media atlas

**430 entries**, each with artwork, exact video sources and confirmed distribution links. The wiki renders artwork and links videos; the interactive library provides in-page players.

[Recommendations](Metroidvanias) | [Interactive players](https://herbertofury.github.io/Games/#hub=Metroidvanias) | [Portable HTML](https://github.com/Herbertofury/Games/releases/latest)
